a <= nthreads; ++a) {\n            if (nthreads % a != 0) continue;\n            const size_t b = nthreads / a;\n            const size_t bz = (nz + a - 1) / a;\n            const size_t by = (ny + b - 1) / b;\n            const size_t cost = (bz + 2) * (by + 2);\n            if (cost < bestCost) {\n                bestCost = cost;\n                pz = a;\n                py = b;\n            }\n        }\n        const size_t rowBytes = std::max<size_t>(nx, 1) * sizeof(Real);\n        tileY = std::max<size_t>(1, (256 * 1024) / (3 * rowBytes));\n    }\n\n    // Range [begin, end) of thread-coordinate i out of p over n elements\n    static void range(const size_t n, const size_t p, const size_t i, size_t& b, size_t& e) {\n        const size_t q = n / p, r = n % p;\n        b = i * q + std::min(i, r);\n        e = b + q + (i < r ? 1 : 0);\n    }\n\n    void block(const size_t tid, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n               size_t& z0, size_t& z1, size_t& y0, size_t& y1) const {\n        range(nz, pz, tid / py, z0, z1);\n        range(ny, py, tid % py, y0, y1);\n    }\n};\n\nvoid initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& other, const Decomposition& dec,\n                    const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict g = grid.data();\n    Real* __restrict o = other.data();\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n        dec.block(omp_get_thread_num(), ny, nz, z0, z1, y0, y1);\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                    o[idx] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation on the rows [y0, y1) x [z0, z1)\nstatic inline void stencilBlock(const Real* __restrict input,\n                                Real* __restrict output,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                const size_t z0, const size_t z1,\n                                const size_t y0, const size_t y1) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const bool zBoundary = (z == 0 || z == nz - 1);\n        for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t row = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            const Real* __restrict in = input + row;\n            Real* __restrict out = output + row;\n            if (zBoundary || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                // Copy boundary row\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) out[x] = in[x];\n                continue;\n            }\n            const Real* __restrict inF = in - nx;\n            const Real* __restrict inB = in + nx;\n            const Real* __restrict inD = in - plane;\n            const Real* __restrict inU = in + plane;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                // Simple averaging stencil (same operation order as the reference)\n                out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + inF[x] + inB[x] + inD[x] + inU[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n            // Copy boundary values in x\n            out[0] = in[0];\n            out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Runs all iterations inside one persistent parallel region; returns the final grid\nconst Grid& runStencil(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2, const Decomposition& dec,\n                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int iterations) {\n    Real* const g1 = grid1.data();\n    Real* const g2 = grid2.data();\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n        dec.block(omp_get_thread_num(), ny, nz, z0, z1, y0, y1);\n        const size_t tileY = dec.tileY;\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? g1 : g2;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? g2 : g1;\n            for (size_t ty = y0; ty < y1; ty += tileY) {\n                stencilBlock(in, out, nx, ny, nz, z0, z1, ty, std::min(y1, ty + tileY));\n            }\n            #pragma omp barrier\n        }\n    }\n    return (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_mid+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <new>\\n#include <utility>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <omp.h>\\n',1)\n\n# validate: parallel\nvs=src.index('bool validateResult'); No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(||: bad) schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) bad = true;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 2. z < z1; ++z) {\n        const bool zBoundary = (z == 0 || z == nz - 1);\n        for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t row = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            const Real* __restrict in = input + row;\n            Real* __restrict out = output + row;\n            if (zBoundary || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                // Copy boundary row\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) out[x] = in[x];\n                continue;\n            }\n            const Real* __restrict inF = in - nx;\n            const Real* __restrict inB = in + nx;\n            const Real* __restrict inD = in - plane;\n            const Real* __restrict inU = in + plane;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                // Simple averaging stencil (same operation order as the reference)\n                out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + inF[x] + inB[x] + inD[x] + inU[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n            // Copy boundary values in x\n            out[0] = in[0];\n            out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Runs all iterations inside one persistent parallel region; returns the final grid\nconst Grid& runStencil(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2, const Decomposition& dec,\n                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int iterations) {\n    Real* const g1 = grid1.data();\n    Real* const g2 = grid2.data();\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n        dec.block(omp_get_thread_num(), ny, nz, z0, z1, y0, y1);\n        const size_t tileY = dec.tileY;\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? g1 : g2;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? g2 : g1;\n            for (size_t ty = y0; ty < y1; ty += tileY) {\n                stencilBlock(in, out, nx, ny, nz, z0, z1, ty, std::min(y1, ty + tileY));\n            }\n            #pragma omp barrier\n        }\n    }\n    return (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new_mid+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <new>\\n#include <utility>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <omp.h>\\n',1)\n\n# validate: parallel\nvs=src.index('bool validateResult'); ve=src.index('void printUsage')\nsrc=src[:vs]+r'''bool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    const Real* g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(||: bad) schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) bad = true;\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = g[0];\n    Real maxVal = g[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(min: minVal) reduction(max: maxVal) schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, g[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, g[i]);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n'''+src[ve:]\n\nold_main=src[src.index('    // Allocate grids'):src.index('    printf(\"Computation time')]\nsrc=src.replace(old_main,r'''    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    Grid grid1(gridSize);\n    Grid grid2(gridSize);\n    const Decomposition dec(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, grid2, dec, nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const Grid& finalGrid = runStencil(grid1, grid2, dec, nx, ny, nz, iterations);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n''')\nsrc=src.replace('''    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? OpenMP threads are unbound by default, which lets the OS\n// migrate them away from the NUMA node holding their first-touched data. Unless the\n// user controls binding via OMP_PLACES / OMP_PROC_BIND, pin thread t to a fixed CPU of\n// the process affinity mask, preferring one hardware thread per physical core.\nstruct ThreadPinning {\n    std::vector<int> cpuOfThread;  // empty: no pinning\n\n    explicit ThreadPinning(const size_t nthreads) {\n        if (getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") || getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\")) return;\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) return;\n        std::vector<int> primary, secondary;\n        for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n            if (!CPU_ISSET(c, &mask)) continue;\n            // First CPU in the sibling list is the core's primary hardware thread\n            std::ifstream f(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu\" + std::to_string(c) + \"/topology/thread_siblings_list\");\n            int first = c;\n            if (f) f >> first;\n            (first == c ? primary : secondary).push_back(c);\n        }\n        if (primary.empty()) return;\n        std::vector<int> all(primary);\n        all.insert(all.end(), secondary.begin(), secondary.end());\n        cpuOfThread.resize(nthreads);\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < nthreads; ++t) {\n            if (nthreads <= primary.size()) {\n                cpuOfThread[t] = primary[t * primary.size() / nthreads];\n            } else {\n                cpuOfThread[t] = all[t % all.size()];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    void pin(const size_t tid) const {\n        if (tid >= cpuOfThread.size()) return;\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpuOfThread[tid], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n};\n\n'''\nsrc=src.replace(anchor,pin+anchor,1)\n# init signature and body\nsrc=src.replace('''void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& other, const Decomposition& dec,''','''void initializeGrid(Grid& grid, Grid& other, const Decomposition& dec, const ThreadPinning& pinning,''')\nsrc=src.replace('''const Grid& runStencil(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2, const Decomposition& dec,''','''const Grid& runStencil(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2, const Decomposition& dec, const ThreadPinning& pinning,''')\nsrc=src.replace('''        size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n        dec.block(omp_get_thread_num(), ny, nz, z0, z1, y0, y1);''','''        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        pinning.pin(tid);\n        size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n        dec.block(tid, ny, nz, z0, z1, y0, y1);''')\nsrc=src.replace('''    const Decomposition dec(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), nx, ny, nz);''','''    const size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    const Decomposition dec(nthreads, nx, ny, nz);\n    const ThreadPinning pinning(nthreads);''')\nsrc=src.replace('initializeGrid(grid1, grid2, dec, nx','initializeGrid(grid1, grid2, dec, pinning, nx')\nsrc=src.replace('runStencil(grid1, grid2, dec, nx','runStencil(grid1, grid2, dec, pinning, nx')\nopen('stencil3d.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ngrep -n \"#include\" stencil3d.cpp; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XKPuTGf6dMumswr6915Zgj","type":"tool_result","content":"                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                    o[idx] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation on the rows [y0, y1) x [z0, z1)\nstatic inline void stencilBlock(const Real* __restrict input,\n                                Real* __restrict output,\n                                const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                                const size_t z0, const size_t z1,\n                                const size_t y0, const size_t y1,\n                                [[maybe_unused]] const bool streaming) {\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;\n    for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const bool zBoundary = (z == 0 || z == nz - 1);\n        for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t row = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            const Real* __restrict in = input + row;\n            Real* __restrict out = output + row;\n            if (zBoundary || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                // Copy boundary row\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) out[x] = in[x];\n                continue;\n            }\n            const Real* __restrict inF = in - nx;\n            const Real* __restrict inB = in + nx;\n            const Real* __restrict inD = in - plane;\n            const Real* __restrict inU = in + plane;\n            size_t x = 1;\n            const size_t xEnd = nx - 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n            // Streaming (non-temporal) stores avoid read-for-ownership traffic on the\n            // output grid; the arithmetic per element is identical to the scalar path.\n            const __m256d seventh = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n            for (; streaming && x < xEnd && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(out + x) & 31) != 0; ++x) {\n                out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + inF[x] + inB[x] + inD[x] + inU[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n            for (; streaming && x + 4 <= xEnd; x += 4) {\n                __m256d s = _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x);\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x - 1));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x + 1));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(inF + x));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(inB + x));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(inD + x));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(inU + x));\n                _mm256_stream_pd(out + x, _mm256_div_pd(s, seventh));\n            }\n#endif\n            for (; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                // Simple averaging stencil (same operation order as the reference)\n                out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + inF[x] + inB[x] + inD[x] + inU[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n            // Copy boundary values in x\n            out[0] = in[0];\n            out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Runs all iterations inside one persistent parallel region; returns the final grid\nconst Grid& runStencil(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2, const Decomposition& dec, const ThreadPinning& pinning,\n                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int iterations) {\n    Real* const g1 = grid1.data();\n    Real* const g2 = grid2.data();\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        pinning.pin(tid);\n        size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n        dec.block(tid, ny, nz, z0, z1, y0, y1);\n        const size_t tileY = dec.tileY;\n        const bool streaming = dec.streaming;\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? g1 : g2;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? g2 : g1;\n            for (size_t ty = y0; ty < y1; ty += tileY) {\n                stencilBlock(in, out, nx, ny, nz, z0, z1, ty, std::min(y1, ty + tileY), streaming);\n            }\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n            _mm_sfence();  // make streaming stores visible before the barrier\n#endif\n            #pragma omp barrier\n        }\n    }\n    return (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    const Real* g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. z < z1; ++z) {\n        const bool zBoundary = (z == 0 || z == nz - 1);\n        for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n            const size_t row = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            const Real* __restrict in = input + row;\n            Real* __restrict out = output + row;\n            if (zBoundary || y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                // Copy boundary row\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) out[x] = in[x];\n                continue;\n            }\n            const Real* __restrict inF = in - nx;\n            const Real* __restrict inB = in + nx;\n            const Real* __restrict inD = in - plane;\n            const Real* __restrict inU = in + plane;\n            size_t x = 1;\n            const size_t xEnd = nx - 1;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n            // Streaming (non-temporal) stores avoid read-for-ownership traffic on the\n            // output grid; the arithmetic per element is identical to the scalar path.\n            const __m256d seventh = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n            for (; streaming && x < xEnd && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(out + x) & 31) != 0; ++x) {\n                out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + inF[x] + inB[x] + inD[x] + inU[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n            for (; streaming && x + 4 <= xEnd; x += 4) {\n                __m256d s = _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x);\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x - 1));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x + 1));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(inF + x));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(inB + x));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(inD + x));\n                s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(inU + x));\n                _mm256_stream_pd(out + x, _mm256_div_pd(s, seventh));\n            }\n#endif\n            for (; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                // Simple averaging stencil (same operation order as the reference)\n                out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + inF[x] + inB[x] + inD[x] + inU[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n            // Copy boundary values in x\n            out[0] = in[0];\n            out[nx - 1] = in[nx - 1];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Runs all iterations inside one persistent parallel region; returns the final grid\nconst Grid& runStencil(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2, const Decomposition& dec, const ThreadPinning& pinning,\n                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int iterations) {\n    Real* const g1 = grid1.data();\n    Real* const g2 = grid2.data();\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        pinning.pin(tid);\n        size_t z0, z1, y0, y1;\n        dec.block(tid, ny, nz, z0, z1, y0, y1);\n        const size_t tileY = dec.tileY;\n        const bool streaming = dec.streaming;\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? g1 : g2;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? g2 : g1;\n            for (size_t ty = y0; ty < y1; ty += tileY) {\n                stencilBlock(in, out, nx, ny, nz, z0, z1, ty, std::min(y1, ty + tileY), streaming);\n            }\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n            _mm_sfence();  // make streaming stores visible before the barrier\n#endif\n            #pragma omp barrier\n        }\n    }\n    return (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    const Real* g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfduLGiQSsXtjDFXV7uCp","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've parallelized stencil3d with OpenMP. On this 2-socket, 128-core EPYC 7763 machine the output is bit-identical to the original (same Sum and Hash), and the 512³ grid at 20 iterations now runs in about 190–250 ms instead of 6945 ms, roughly 30× faster.\n\n**Testing:** I compared Sum and Hash against the original build on odd and very small grids (including 1³, 2×3×4 and 0 iterations) at 1, 7, 200 and 256 threads. All matched, and `-v` validation passes.\n\n**Scaling** (512³, 50 iterations, million cell updates per second):\n\n| threads | 1 | 16 | 32 | 64 | 128 | 256 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| MCUP/s | 635 | 8,558 | 12,192 | 13,481 | 13,591 | 12,125 |\n\nIt stops improving at about 64 threads because memory bandwidth runs out. Each iteration moves about 3 GB, which at this speed is roughly 240 GB/s, close to what the machine can deliver. Timings were noisy because the machine was busy during testing (load average about 47).\n\n**What changed in `stencil3d.cpp`:**\n- **One parallel region for all iterations:** threads stay alive and wait at a barrier between iterations instead of being restarted each time. The edge-copy pass is merged into the main loop, so each row is handled in one go.\n- **Fixed work split:** each thread always gets the same block of (z, y) rows, and both the initialization and every iteration use that split. Rows are processed in small groups that fit in each core's L2 cache, which cuts repeated reads from memory.\n- **Memory placement on the right socket:** the grids now use an aligned allocator that doesn't zero memory up front. Each thread fills its own block during initialization, so that memory lands on the socket where the thread runs.\n- **Thread pinning:** by default OpenMP doesn't pin threads here, so the OS moved them around and they lost contact with their data. The program now pins each thread to a fixed CPU within whatever CPUs it's allowed to use, preferring one thread per physical core. This took the default 128³ grid at 100 iterations from about 280 ms to 16 ms. If you set `OMP_PLACES` or `OMP_PROC_BIND` yourself, the program uses your settings instead.\n- **Faster writes for large grids:** when the two grids together exceed 1 GiB, the inner loop writes results straight to memory without first loading them into cache (AVX streaming stores), worth about 20%. Below that size it uses the normal loop, because I measured streaming as slower when the grids fit in cache. The calculation per cell is unchanged and in the original order, so results stay bit-identical.\n- **Validation** now runs in parallel. For `-r`, the result is copied into a plain `std::vector` because the shared `print_results` helper requires one; this happens after the timed section.\n\n**Caveat:** with all 256 threads on this machine, the default 128³ run with 10 iterations took 49 ms against 49 ms serial, before pinning was added. I didn't re-time that exact run afterwards. The pinned 100-iteration run (16 ms) suggests it's much faster now, but I haven't measured it.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`** now requires OpenMP (`find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)`) and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. The executable is still called `stencil3d`, no new files were added, and there are no new external dependencies."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2723,"cache_read_input_tokens":50587,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2723},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e0cf33a0-7282-4193-a4b1-8b6e3151c214","uuid":"0b4b5947-a0c5-464c-a2d0-38330b0790c6","timestamp":"2026-10-02T18:43:19.736Z","request_id":"req_011CfduLGJMP6PLNc5ftv33d"}
